<script id="bdrc-living-bootstrap">
(function (param, cookieName, cssClass) {
	var TRUE = /^(1|true|yes|on)$/i;
	function readCookie() {
		var m = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + cookieName + '=([^;]*)'));
		return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
	}
	function writeCookie(value) {
		var base = cookieName + '=' + value + ';path=/;samesite=lax' +
			(location.protocol === 'https:' ? ';secure' : '');
		document.cookie = value === '' ? base + ';max-age=0' : base; // no max-age = session cookie
	}
	var on;
	var requested = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get(param);
	if (requested !== null) {
		on = TRUE.test(requested);
		on ? writeCookie('1') : writeCookie('');
	} else {
		on = TRUE.test(readCookie() || '');
	}
	document.documentElement.classList.toggle(cssClass, on);
})("living", "bdrc_living", "bdrc-living");
</script>

/* --- BDRC "living" header skin — palette shared with the landing page --- */
html.bdrc-living {
	--bdrc-brand: #8b1e2b;
	--bdrc-brand-hover: #a42a39;

	--bdrc-cream: #fbf4e8;
	--bdrc-gold: #e5a832;
	--bdrc-gold-soft: #f3c76b;
	/* Page-body palette (used by the home skin below) */
	/* Light ochre, not the design's #f0dfaf — the one token that departs from it,
	   on request after seeing the yellow in place. */
	--bdrc-sand: #f4ebdc;
	--bdrc-paper: #fbf8f2;
	--bdrc-card: #fffefa;
	--bdrc-ink: #2a1512;
	--bdrc-body-text: #4a3128;
	--bdrc-muted: #6b4a3c;
	--bdrc-line: rgba(42, 18, 18, 0.13);

/* Header bar */
html.bdrc-living header .header_bottom,
html.bdrc-living header.sticky .header_bottom,
html.bdrc-living header.fixed.scrolled .header_bottom,
html.bdrc-living header.fixed_hiding.scrolled .header_bottom {
	background-color: var(--bdrc-maroon) !important;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(229, 168, 50, 0.25);
	box-shadow: none !important;
}

/* Logo: the stock PNG is dark, knock it out to cream on the maroon bar */
html.bdrc-living .q_logo a img {
	filter: brightness(0) invert(1);
}

/* --- Header bar layout ---
   The theme centres the menu with the old "absolute at 50%, pull the ul back by
   half its width" trick, which leaves a gap between the last item and the Donate
   button. The design has the nav right-aligned against it, so the bar becomes a
   flex row: logo, menu (flexible, right-aligned), widget.

   `.container_inner.clearfix` has ::before/::after clearfix pseudo-elements, and
   in a flex container those become flex items — they have to be switched off or
   they push the logo away from the edge.

   Desktop only: below the theme's 1000px breakpoint the header switches to its
   own mobile layout (burger button, logo positioned differently), which the flex
   row breaks. */
@media only screen and (min-width: 1001px) {
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container_inner {
		display: flex !important;
		align-items: center !important;
		gap: 28px;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container_inner::before,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container_inner::after {
		display: none !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container_inner > * {
		float: none !important;
	}
	/* The design's bar is 14px of padding around a 50px logo — 78px. The theme
	   hard-codes 105px with a 55px logo. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .container_inner {
		height: 78px !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_inner_left,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_inner_right,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .main_menu,
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .logo_wrapper {
		height: auto !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_inner_left {
		position: relative !important;
		order: 1;
		flex: 0 0 auto;
	}
	/* `.logo_wrapper` is a fixed-height block the logo sits at the top of; the
	   theme centres it with `top: 50%` on .q_logo paired with a negative `top`
	   on the image, and that pairing breaks once the image is back in flow. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .logo_wrapper {
		display: flex !important;
		align-items: center !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .q_logo {
		top: auto !important;
		margin-top: 0 !important;
		transform: none !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .main_menu {
		position: static !important;
		left: auto !important;
		width: auto !important;
		order: 2;
		flex: 1 1 auto;
		min-width: 0;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_inner_right {
		order: 3;
		flex: 0 0 auto;
		left: 0 !important;
		right: auto !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom nav.mobile_menu {
		order: 4;
	}
	/* The logo image is absolutely positioned with a negative top to centre it;
	   flex does that on its own once it is back in flow. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .q_logo a {
		display: block !important;
		height: 50px !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .q_logo a img {
		position: static !important;
		top: auto !important;
		left: auto !important;
		display: block !important;
		height: 50px !important;
		width: auto !important;
		margin: 0 !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .main_menu > ul {
		position: static !important;
		left: auto !important;
		width: auto !important;
		display: flex !important;
		justify-content: flex-end;
		align-items: center;
		flex-wrap: wrap;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .main_menu > ul > li {
		float: none !important;
	}
	/* Nav link scale from the design: 13.5px / 600 / 0.1em, 9px 13px padding. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .main_menu > ul > li > a {
		font-size: 13.5px !important;
		font-weight: 600 !important;
		letter-spacing: 0.1em !important;
		line-height: 1.2 !important;
		height: auto !important;
		padding: 9px 13px !important;
		border-radius: 2px;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .main_menu > ul > li > a > span {
		line-height: 1.2 !important;
		height: auto !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .main_menu > ul > li:hover > a {
		background-color: rgba(229, 168, 50, 0.14) !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_bottom_right_widget_holder .qbutton {
		font-size: 12.5px !important;
		letter-spacing: 0.14em !important;
		line-height: 1.2 !important;
		height: auto !important;
		padding: 11px 20px !important;
	}
	/* In the design both bars end on the same 32px gutter: the last link of the
	   utility strip and the donation button share a right edge. The theme keeps
	   17px of padding on the widget holder, which stopped the button short of it
	   — the strip's "donate" landed at 1393 and the button at 1376. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_bottom .header_bottom_right_widget_holder {
		padding-right: 0 !important;
	}
}

/* --- Utility strip above the header ---
   The theme renders no `.header_top`, so this markup is created client-side (see
   print_runtime_js) and only ever exists when the skin is on. */
html .bdrc-living-topbar {
	display: none;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar {
	display: block;
	background-color: var(--bdrc-brand);
	color: rgba(255, 250, 245, 0.92);
	font-family: var(--bdrc-sans-font);
	font-size: 12px;
	letter-spacing: 0.08em;
	/* The theme's global `line-height: 26px` would otherwise make the strip 40px
	   tall instead of the design's ~29px. */
	line-height: 1.25;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__tagline,
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links {
	line-height: 1.25;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__inner {
	max-width: 1440px;
	margin: 0 auto;
	padding: 7px 32px;
	box-sizing: border-box;
	display: flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: space-between;
	gap: 24px;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__tagline {
	font-style: italic;
	opacity: 0.9;
	flex: 1;
	min-width: 0;
	white-space: nowrap;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links {
	display: flex;
	align-items: center;
	gap: 22px;
	flex-shrink: 0;
	text-transform: uppercase;
	letter-spacing: 0.12em;
	font-weight: 600;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links a {
	color: rgba(255, 250, 245, 0.92) !important;
	text-decoration: none;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links a:hover {
	color: var(--bdrc-gold) !important;
}
html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links a.is-donate {
	color: var(--bdrc-gold-soft) !important;
	font-weight: 700;
}
/* Uppercasing, letter-spacing and italics are Latin-script conventions: they do
   nothing in Chinese and actively break Tibetan, which stacks its glyphs.
   WPML sets the language on <html> (bo-CN, zh-hans). */
html.bdrc-living[lang^="bo"] .bdrc-living-topbar__links,
html.bdrc-living[lang^="zh"] .bdrc-living-topbar__links {
	text-transform: none;
	letter-spacing: normal;
}
html.bdrc-living[lang^="bo"] .bdrc-living-topbar__tagline,
html.bdrc-living[lang^="zh"] .bdrc-living-topbar__tagline {
	font-style: normal;
	letter-spacing: normal;
}
/* Tibetan needs vertical room for its stacks. */
html.bdrc-living[lang^="bo"] .bdrc-living-topbar {
	font-size: 13px;
	line-height: 1.9;
}
/* --- Hauteur réservée sous l'en-tête fixe ---
   The skin shrinks the bar from the theme's 105px to 78 in CSS, so that lands on
   the first paint; but the margin the theme pins `.content` down with — 105px,
   `!important` — was only corrected by `syncOffset()` after `DOMContentLoaded`.
   Between the two, a 26px strip of page background showed under the header and
   then vanished. The expected height is therefore declared as a token here, so
   the first paint is already right; `syncOffset()` still measures afterwards and
   corrects the value if the real height differs.
   Two values, both measured rather than derived from the declarations: 79px of
   main bar plus the strip, which comes out at 29px in English and Chinese and
   24px in Tibetan — the taller line-height there buys vertical room inside a
   strip that ends up shorter, not taller, than the latin one. The token is
   per-language because CSS cannot measure; the language is in the URL, so this
   is safe under full-page caching, unlike the skin toggle itself.
   Breakpoint matched to the theme's own: below 1000px it zeroes the margin and
   the header returns to normal flow. */
html.bdrc-living {
	--bdrc-topbar-h: 29px;
	--bdrc-header-h: 108px;
}
html.bdrc-living[lang^="bo"] {
	--bdrc-topbar-h: 24px;
	--bdrc-header-h: 103px;
}
@media only screen and (min-width: 1001px) {
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .content.content_top_margin {
		margin-top: var(--bdrc-header-h) !important;
	}
	/* …and the header has to be that tall from the first paint too, or the space
	   reserved below it shows through as a strip until the strip itself mounts.
	   A first version only set the margin, which traded the theme's 26px gap for
	   a 29px one — the check that passed was made *after* mounting, where the two
	   heights agree by construction.
	   `:has()` is what makes it self-cancelling: while the strip is absent, the
	   holder stands in for it — same height, same brand background, in the same
	   place. The moment the strip is inserted the selector stops matching and the
	   padding goes, so nothing moves. Browsers without `:has()` fall back to the
	   old transient gap, which is what they had before. */
	html.bdrc-living .header_top_bottom_holder:not(:has(.bdrc-living-topbar)) {
		padding-top: var(--bdrc-topbar-h);
		background-color: var(--bdrc-brand);
	}
}
html.bdrc-living[lang^="bo"] .bdrc-living-topbar__inner {
	padding-top: 4px;
	padding-bottom: 4px;
}
@media only screen and (max-width: 768px) {
	html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__tagline {
		display: none;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__inner {
		justify-content: center;
		padding-left: 16px;
		padding-right: 16px;
	}
	html.bdrc-living .bdrc-living-topbar__links {
		gap: 16px;
		font-size: 11px;
	}
}

/* --- Pictograms repainted on light bands (see `repaintPictos()`) ---
   The design does not use these pictograms at all: it has a 30px Material Symbols
   glyph in brand red above each card title. Closest transposition with the assets
   the site actually has — the theme's own shape, at the design's scale and colour,
   rather than a 128px ring. */

	display: block;
	width: 40px;
	height: 40px;
	background-color: var(--bdrc-brand);
	-webkit-mask-repeat: no-repeat;
	mask-repeat: no-repeat;
	-webkit-mask-position: center;
	mask-position: center;
	-webkit-mask-size: contain;
	mask-size: contain;
}

   inside these blocks, which disappears once the band is light. */

}

	color: var(--bdrc-ink) !important;
}
/* --- Light-band icon blocks, laid out as the design's cards ---
   The design stacks them: the glyph on its own line, then a Prata title, then an
   outlined button. The theme instead floats a pictogram to the left and pushes the
   copy over with a 160px padding, which left the icon marooned beside the text.
   The card itself (`#FFFEFA`, 1px border, 3px radius, 30px padding) already comes
   from the page rules and matches the design. */

	float: none !important;
	width: auto !important;

	padding-left: 0 !important;
	width: auto !important;
}

   sized up until the symbol inside reads at the design's weight. */

	width: 44px;
	height: 44px;
}

	font-size: 24px !important;
	line-height: 1.2 !important;
	/* the theme's own top margin added up with the icon's, giving 32px where the
	   design has about 12 */
	margin-top: 0 !important;
}
/* The design carries the whole sentence in one 24px Prata heading — « Learn how
   you can support BDRC ». The theme splits it across an `h5` and a `<p>`, so the

/* --- Inner-page hero ---
   About, Programs, Archive and Donate all open with the same construct: a two
   cell holder, the photo as a background-image on the left cell and the title in
   a `.page-title-bloc` on the right, with `h1.small-title` above it playing the
   design's gold eyebrow. `:has()` keeps this off the home page, whose hero is
   built differently and has its own rules.

   The photo stays on its cell rather than moving to the row, so no image URL has
   to be hard-coded; the row goes maroon behind it and a gradient on the cell's
   own ::after blends the two. */
html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) {
	background-color: var(--bdrc-maroon) !important;
}

   light grey. Clearing the colour leaves the photo cell's background-image. */
html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_item {
	background-color: transparent !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_item[style*="background-image"] {
	position: relative;
	background-size: cover !important;
	background-position: center !important;
}
/* .q_elements_item::after is a theme clearfix — reset every box property. */
html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_item[style*="background-image"]::after {
	content: '';
	display: block;
	clear: none;
	position: absolute;
	top: 0;
	right: 0;
	bottom: 0;
	left: 0;
	width: auto;
	height: auto;
	pointer-events: none;
	background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(52,16,16,0.10) 0%, rgba(52,16,16,0.35) 55%, rgba(52,16,16,0.95) 100%);
}
/* --- Photo bleeding to the left edge ---
   The photo cell started 55px in — 40px of section gutter plus the theme's 15px
   column padding — so a strip of maroon sat between the viewport edge and the
   image. The gutters are dropped on this row only, the holder swaps its
   `display:table` for flex (a table-cell cannot be bled out with a negative
   margin), and the title cell takes the inset back so the copy keeps its margin.
   Desktop only: below 1000px the theme stacks the two cells on its own. */
@media only screen and (min-width: 1001px) {
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) > .full_section_inner {
		max-width: none !important;
		padding-left: 0 !important;
		padding-right: 0 !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column > .vc_column-inner {
		padding-left: 0 !important;
		padding-right: 0 !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_holder.two_columns {
		display: flex !important;
		/* the theme centres its cells, which left the photo at its content height
		   (250px, the height of a `vc_empty_space`) instead of the band's 398 —
		   the table layout used to equalise them for free */
		align-items: stretch !important;
		width: 100% !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_holder.two_columns > .q_elements_item {
		display: block !important;
		width: 50% !important;
		float: none !important;
		/* the cell carries a height of its own, and `stretch` only applies to items
		   whose height is `auto` — without this the photo stayed 250px tall while
		   the band was 398 */
		height: auto !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .q_elements_item_inner {
		width: 100% !important;
		box-sizing: border-box;
	}
	/* The copy keeps the design's gutter. On the content box only: the theme's
	   `.q_elements_item_inner` is a 100%-wide `content-box`, so padding added
	   there pushed the block 88px past the cell. */
	html.bdrc-living body.bdrc-skin .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1):has(.page-title-bloc) .page-title-bloc .q_elements_item_content {
		box-sizing: border-box;
		max-width: 100%;
		padding-left: 48px !important;
		padding-right: 40px !important;
	}
}

	position: relative;
	z-index: 2;

	font-size: 12px !important;
	font-weight: 700 !important;

	letter-spacing: 0.22em !important;

/* --- 1. Hero: photo + right-hand gradient, text knocked out in cream --- */
/* The design's hero is a 540px-min band with 80px of padding and the text block
   centred vertically. The row's own padding (100/130) plus the ~30px the title
   holder brings of its own overshot that, so the padding is re-set here and the
   band is centred with flexbox — `.full_section_inner` becomes the single flex
   item, and the gradient `::after` stays out of flow because it is absolute. */
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1) {
	position: relative;
	background-position: center top !important;
	background-size: cover !important;
	padding-top: 50px !important;
	padding-bottom: 50px !important;
	min-height: 540px;
	box-sizing: border-box;
	display: flex;
	align-items: center;
}
/* The theme already uses .vc_row::after as a clearfix (display:table;clear:both),
   which collapses it to 0x0 — every box property has to be reset explicitly. */
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(1)::after {
	content: '';
	display: block;
	clear: none;
	position: absolute;
	top: 0;
	right: 0;
	bottom: 0;
	left: 0;
	width: auto;
	height: auto;
	pointer-events: none;
	background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(52,16,16,0.10) 0%, rgba(52,16,16,0.18) 40%, rgba(52,16,16,0.62) 76%, rgba(52,16,16,0.88) 100%);
}

	position: relative;
	z-index: 2;
	width: 100%;
}
/* On desktop the title sits in a 2-cell table the theme sizes to 855px; stretch
   it so the hard-coded <br>s in the H1 do not overflow at 52px. Below that the
   holder stacks on its own, so leave the widths alone. */
@media only screen and (min-width: 1000px) {

		width: 46% !important;

/* the theme paints a solid white card behind the title — drop it */

/* --- Side padding on the rows themselves ---
   Some rows carry a percentage side padding of their own (the people band, for
   one, has `padding-left:10%` — 142px at 1440), which pushes the whole section
   right and leaves it off-centre. The measure comes from `.full_section_inner`,
   so the rows are flattened. Vertical padding is left alone. */
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row {
	padding-left: 0 !important;
	padding-right: 0 !important;
}

/* --- Left edge of the content ---
   The design lines every section up on the same 40px gutter. Three things were
   pulling against it: the theme's 15px column padding (edge at 55), the -15px
   gutter every nested `.vc_row` carries (edge at 25, and 40 when the two happen
   to cancel out), and rows whose own percentage padding is zeroed just above.
   Nested rows lose their gutter — `page_css()` only zeroes it on the top-level
   ones — and the outermost columns lose their padding on the outer side only,
   so the space between columns is kept. */
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner .vc_row {
	margin-left: 0 !important;
	margin-right: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:first-child > .vc_column-inner {
	padding-left: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:last-child > .vc_column-inner {
	padding-right: 0 !important;
}

/* --- Clearfix pseudo-elements in the restructured sections ---
   `.full_section_inner` carries `clearfix`, whose `::before` / `::after` become
   flex or grid items as soon as the box is turned into one — they eat a cell in
   the grid and two extra gaps in the flex rows. Same trap as in the header and
   the news grid. */

html.bdrc-living body.home .full_width_inner > .vc_row:nth-child(2) > .full_section_inner::after,
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875298541 > .full_section_inner::before,
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875298541 > .full_section_inner::after,
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner::before,

	display: none !important;

/* --- 11. Programs --- */
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 {
	background-color: var(--bdrc-paper) !important;
	padding-top: 88px !important;
}
/* The design has two columns: title and copy on the left, photo on the right.
   The live page nests them one level deeper — a 1/5 column holding nothing but
   the title, then a 4/5 column holding an inner row of copy and photo — so the
   title ends up in a 270px gutter to the left of everything.
   The chain between the 4/5 column and its two children is set to
   `display:contents`, which promotes the copy and the photo into the outer
   grid; from there the three cells can be placed as the design has them. The
   nested `.full_section_inner` is a clearfix, and its pseudo-elements would
   become grid items, so they have to go. */
@media only screen and (min-width: 1000px) {
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner {
		display: grid;
		grid-template-columns: 46.5% 1fr;
		column-gap: 64px;
		align-items: center;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:nth-child(2),
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:nth-child(2) > .vc_column-inner,
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:nth-child(2) > .vc_column-inner > .wpb_wrapper,
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:nth-child(2) .vc_row.vc_inner,
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:nth-child(2) .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner {
		display: contents;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner::before,
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner::after {
		display: none !important;
	}
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .wpb_column {
		float: none !important;
		width: auto !important;
	}
	/* title */
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:first-child {
		grid-column: 1;
		grid-row: 1;
		align-self: end;
	}
	/* copy, promoted out of the inner row */
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:first-child {
		grid-column: 1;
		grid-row: 2;
	}
	/* photo, spanning both rows of the right-hand column */
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:last-child {
		grid-column: 2;
		grid-row: 1 / span 2;
	}

		padding-left: 0 !important;
		padding-right: 0 !important;

	/* the design's photo fills the right-hand cell */
	html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1701875245581 .vc_row.vc_inner > .full_section_inner > .wpb_column:last-child img {

/* --- 12. People & partners --- */

/* The "people" widget insets its own content by 80px on each side; the design
   has the column flush with the gutter.
   Addressed by position, not by the widget's own `q_elements_holder_custom_*`
   class: unlike the `vc_custom_*` ones, that number is **regenerated on every
   render** (two captures of the same page give two different sets), so a rule
   pinned to one number silently stops matching. The text column is the second
   of the four in the inner row; the partners grid is a different one and keeps
   its inset. */

	border-radius: 3px;
}
/* --- Pagination of the partners grid ---
   The plugin builds its own round buttons out of pseudo-elements — `::before` on
   the previous link, `::after` on the next one — with a WPBakery icon font that
   *is* loaded, and it paints the `←` / `→` characters transparent underneath. The
   only thing wrong was the palette: a 2px navy border on a sand band, which is
   what read as a stray glyph.
   So this is a recolour, not a replacement. A first attempt drew its own chevrons
   here and overrode `content`, which collided with the plugin's own pseudos and
   left the next arrow empty — the page numbers are the plugin's business too, it
   hides them with `display:none !important` in AJAX mode. */
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1580396415488 .page-navi a.prev-ajax::before,
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1580396415488 .page-navi a.next-ajax::after {
	border: 1px solid rgba(139, 30, 43, 0.35) !important;
	border-radius: 50% !important;
	padding: 11px !important;
	line-height: 1 !important;
	color: var(--bdrc-brand) !important;
	background-color: var(--bdrc-card) !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1580396415488 .page-navi a.prev-ajax:hover::before,
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1580396415488 .page-navi a.next-ajax:hover::after {
	background-color: var(--bdrc-brand) !important;
	border-color: var(--bdrc-brand) !important;
	color: var(--bdrc-cream) !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.home .vc_custom_1580396415488 .page-navi a.disable-click {
	opacity: 0.35;
}

/* Each entry is a `.wpb_text_column.locations` holding `<em>City</em>` and a
   `<strong>Country</strong>`; the design turns them into a ruled list with the
   city in italic Prata and the country as a small brand-red label. */

	line-height: 1.2;
	color: var(--bdrc-ink) !important;
}
/* Anatomy of an entry: `<p><em>City</em><strong><br></strong><strong>Country</strong></p>`
   — the first `<strong>` holds nothing but the line break. Making every `strong`
   a block turned that one into an empty line, which is where the gap between city
   and country came from. The break goes, and only the last `strong` — the country
   — takes the label treatment. Entries with no city (there is one) still work:
   their only `strong` is also the last one. */

	display: block;
	font-size: 12px !important;
	font-weight: 700 !important;
	line-height: 1.4;
	text-transform: uppercase;
	letter-spacing: 0.14em;
	color: var(--bdrc-brand) !important;
}

/* --- Footer (site-wide, pairs with the header skin) --- */
/* The theme's `.footer_inner` is a fixed 1150px column, so the dark band stopped
   short of both edges on the home page, where the wrapper is full-bleed. Letting
   the holders span their wrapper and re-centring the content inside them gives
   the design's edge-to-edge footer. Elsewhere the wrapper is still 1150, so
   nothing moves. */
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_inner,
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_top,
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_top_holder,
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_bottom_holder {
	width: 100% !important;
	max-width: none !important;
}
/* `.footer_top` has no `.container_inner`, so its `.container` is the one that
   has to carry the design's 1440/40 measure. */
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_top .container,
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_bottom_holder .container {
	width: 100% !important;
	max-width: 1440px !important;
	box-sizing: border-box;
	padding-left: 40px !important;
	padding-right: 40px !important;
	margin-left: auto !important;
	margin-right: auto !important;
}
/* …and then the inner one, when there is one, must not add a second measure. */
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_bottom_holder .container .container_inner {
	width: 100% !important;
	max-width: none !important;
	padding-left: 0 !important;
	padding-right: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_top_holder,
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_bottom_holder {
	background-color: var(--bdrc-ink) !important;
}
/* Two theme quirks knocked the footer off the design's 1440/40 measure:
   `.footer_top .container_inner` carries a percentage side margin (33.6px at
   1440) on top of its own 40px padding, so the content started at 74 instead of
   40; and `.footer_bottom_holder` carries the same `padding-left:10%` as the
   people band — 142px — which pushed the bottom bar right and made it 143px
   wider than the viewport. */
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_top .container_inner {
	width: 100% !important;
	max-width: none !important;
	margin-left: auto !important;
	margin-right: auto !important;
	padding-left: 0 !important;
	padding-right: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living footer .footer_bottom_holder {
	padding-left: 0 !important;
	padding-right: 0 !important;
}

/* --- The card grid ---
   The theme ships the posts as full-width rows, each split internally into three
   columns (title+date | excerpt | image). The design wants a three-column grid of
   stacked cards, so the outer list becomes the grid and each item's internal row
   is turned upright.

   Three things have to be undone first, and each is invisible until it bites:
   `.vc_pageable-slide-wrapper` carries `vc_clearfix`, whose ::before/::after
   become grid items and push the first card out of column one; `.vc_gitem_row`
   is absolutely positioned, which collapses every card to 2px once it is the
   thing being sized; and hidden items still occupy grid cells. */
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_grid.vc_row {
	margin-left: 0 !important;
	margin-right: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_pageable-slide-wrapper {
	display: grid !important;
	grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr) !important;
	gap: 28px !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_pageable-slide-wrapper::before,
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_pageable-slide-wrapper::after {
	display: none !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_pageable-slide-wrapper > .vc_grid-item {
	width: 100% !important;
	height: auto !important;
	float: none !important;
	padding: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_pageable-slide-wrapper > .vc_grid-item:not(.vc_visible-item) {
	display: none !important;
}
/* Un-position the theme's inner scaffolding. */
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-zone,
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-animated-block {
	height: auto !important;
	min-height: 0 !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-zone-mini {
	position: static !important;
	height: auto !important;
	width: 100% !important;
}
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem_row,
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-row-position-top,
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-row-position-middle,
html.bdrc-living body.page-id-35 .vc_gitem-row-position-bottom {
	position: static !important;
	transform: none !important;
	top: auto !important;
	height: auto !important;
	margin: 0 !important;

			class="vc_empty_space_inner">
			<span class="empty_space_image"  ></span>

</div></div></div><div class='q_elements_item page-title-bloc' data-768-1024='30px 30px 30px 30px' data-600-768='30px 30px 30px 0px' data-480-600='30px 30px 30px 0px' data-480='30px 30px 30px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_220161' style='vertical-align:middle;text-align:left;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_220161' style='padding:80px 80px 100px 80px'><h1 style="text-align: left" class="vc_custom_heading small-title" >About Us</h1><h1 style="text-align: left" class="vc_custom_heading" >Buddhist Digital<br />
Resource Center</h1>
	<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element  subtitle">
		<div class="wpb_wrapper">
			<p>Preserving Buddhist Literature for the World</p>

		</div> 
	</div> </div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix" style='padding: 0% 10%'><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-1/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder one_column responsive_mode_from_1300' ><div class='q_elements_item ' data-768-1024='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-600-768='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480-600='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_548398' style='vertical-align:top;text-align:left;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_548398' style='padding:80px 0px 0px 0px'><h2 style="font-size: 15px;line-height: 22px;text-align: left" class="vc_custom_heading intro-title" >What We Do</h2></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-4/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix" style='padding: 0% 0%'><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder one_column responsive_mode_from_1300' ><div class='q_elements_item ' data-768-1024='25px 0px 40px 0px' data-600-768='25px 0px 40px 0px' data-480-600='25px 0px 40px 0px' data-480='25px 0px 40px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_671472' style='vertical-align:top;text-align:left;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_671472' style='padding:80px 0px 80px 0px'>
	<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element  intro">
		<div class="wpb_wrapper">
			<p>The Buddhist Digital Resource Center is a nonprofit organization dedicated to seeking out, preserving, documenting, and disseminating Buddhist literature. We provide scholars, translators, Buddhist practitioners, and the general public with access to an unparalleled collection of Buddhist texts. Joining digital technology with scholarship, BDRC ensures that the cultural treasures of  the Buddhist literary tradition are secure and accessible for generations to come.</p>

		</div> 
	</div> </div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid  icon-section disable_negative_margin" vc_col-lg-1/5 vc_col-md-1"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"></div></div></div><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-4/5 vc_col-md-11"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-6"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder one_column responsive_mode_from_768' ><div class='q_elements_item data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_932188'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_932188' style='padding:10px 0px 10px 20px'><div decoding="async" itemprop="image" style="" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/02/LIBRARY.svg" alt=""></div><div class="icon_text_holder" style=""><div class="icon_text_inner" vc_col-lg-1/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder one_column responsive_mode_from_1300' ><div class='q_elements_item ' data-768-1024='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-600-768='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480-600='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_533760' style='vertical-align:top;text-align:left;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_533760' vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-4/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div data-480='25px 0px 0px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_977973' style='vertical-align:top;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_977973' style='padding:90px 20px 80px 0px'>
	<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element ">
		<div class="wpb_wrapper">
			<p>Our international team carries out BDRC's mission with ingenuity and dedication, using its skills to make BDRC the online home for Buddhist literature.</p>

		</div> 
	</div> 	<div class="vc_empty_space"  style="height: 32px" ><span
			class="vc_empty_space_inner">
			<span class="empty_space_image"  ></span>
		</span></div>

<a  itemprop="url" href="https://www.bdrc.io/people/" target="_self"  class="qbutton  default" style="text-transform: uppercase; ">Discover the people involved</a>	<div class="vc_empty_space"  style="height: 32px" ><span
			class="vc_empty_space_inner">
			<span class="empty_space_image"  ></span>
		</span></div>

</div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid " style=' padding-top:80px; padding-bottom:40px; text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><h2 style="text-align: center" class="vc_custom_heading vc_custom_1579371871855" >A Brief History of BDRC</h2><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_hidden-xs"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper">	<div class="vc_empty_space"  style="height: 32px" ><span
			class="vc_empty_space_inner">
			<span class="empty_space_image"  ></span>
		</span></div>

</div></div></div></div></div><div class="qode-workflow ">
    <span class="main-line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner ">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image left">
            <img fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" width="500" height="500" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Gene-Smith-at-Home-NewDelhi-1982.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="Gene Smith at Home - New Delhi 1982" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Gene-Smith-at-Home-NewDelhi-1982.jpg 500w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Gene-Smith-at-Home-NewDelhi-1982-300x300.jpg 300w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Gene-Smith-at-Home-NewDelhi-1982-150x150.jpg 150w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 100vw, 500px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>1997</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">BDRC Founder E. Gene Smith realizes computer technology can preserve and disseminate texts on an unprecedented scale. He takes early retirement from the Library of Congress to fulfill his lifelong dream of making Tibetan texts widely accessible.<br />
{Read E. Gene Smith's biography}(https://www.bdrc.io/people/e-gene-smith/)</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner reverse">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img decoding="async" width="349" height="135" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/TBRC_logo.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="TBRC Logo" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/TBRC_logo.jpg 349w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/TBRC_logo-300x116.jpg 300w" sizes="(max-width: 349px) 100vw, 349px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>1999</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">Gene Smith starts the Tibetan Buddhist Resource Center (TBRC) in his home in Cambridge, Massachusetts. TBRC begins digitizing Gene's personal collection of Tibetan texts, and making the scanned images available on CDs.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner ">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img decoding="async" width="181" height="275" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Among-Tibetan-Texts-Gene-Smith-book.png" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="Among Tibetan Texts Gene Smith&#039;s book" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2001</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">Gene Smith's book, Among Tibetan Texts, is published.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner reverse">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="354" height="236" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Jeff-Wallman-TBRC-Executive-Director.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="Jeff Wallman BDRC Executive Director Emeritus" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Jeff-Wallman-TBRC-Executive-Director.jpg 354w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Jeff-Wallman-TBRC-Executive-Director-300x200.jpg 300w" sizes="auto, (max-width: 354px) 100vw, 354px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2009</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">Jeff Wallman, TBRC's Director of Technology, becomes Executive Director.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner ">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="225" height="332" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Digital-Dharma-documentary-save-Tibetan-literary-heritage.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="Digital Dharma Dafna Yachin's documentary" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Digital-Dharma-documentary-save-Tibetan-literary-heritage.jpg 225w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/Digital-Dharma-documentary-save-Tibetan-literary-heritage-203x300.jpg 203w" sizes="auto, (max-width: 225px) 100vw, 225px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2012</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">Lunchbox Communications releases Digital Dharma, Dafna Yachin's full-length documentary about Gene Smith's quest to save Tibetan literary heritage.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner reverse">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="2560" height="1714" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-scaled.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="Text at Fragile Palm Leaves Foundation" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-scaled.jpg 2560w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-300x201.jpg 300w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-1024x685.jpg 1024w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-768x514.jpg 768w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-1536x1028.jpg 1536w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-2048x1371.jpg 2048w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/07/DSC_0109-700x469.jpg 700w" sizes="auto, (max-width: 2560px) 100vw, 2560px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2015</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">The TBRC Board of Directors votes to expand the organization's mission beyond Tibetan materials to include the preservation of texts from all Buddhist traditions.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>
    <div class="qode-workflow-item-inner ">
        <div class="qode-workflow-image center">
            <img loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="1263" height="368" src="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo.jpg" class="attachment-full size-full" alt="BDRC Logo" srcset="https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo.jpg 1263w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo-300x87.jpg 300w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo-1024x298.jpg 1024w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo-768x224.jpg 768w, https://www.bdrc.io/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Buddhist_Digital_Resource_Center_Logo-700x204.jpg 700w" sizes="auto, (max-width: 1263px) 100vw, 1263px" />        </div>
        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2016</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">TBRC officially changes its name to the Buddhist Digital Resource Center (BDRC) and launches its first major digital preservation initiative in Southeast Asia at the Fragile Palm Leaves archive while also continuing its Tibetan literary heritage projects.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>

                            <h3>2017</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">BDRC begins developing its next-generation archival platform, the Buddhist Digital Archives (BUDA).</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>

                            <h3>2018</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">Jann Ronis succeeds Jeff Wallman as Executive Director. {Read Jann Ronis's biography}(https://www.bdrc.io/jann-ronis/)</p>

        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>2021</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">BDRC releases its new archival platform, the Buddhist Universal Digital Archive (BUDA), and retires the tbrc.org website. As well as being the world's largest digital library of Buddhist texts, BUDA is a Linked Open Data archival platform, a project to innovate and share technology and research tools for scholars, translators, and practitioners.</p>
                    </div>
    </div>
</div><div class="qode-workflow-item">
    <span class="line" style="background-color:#d73449;"></span>

        <div class="qode-workflow-text">
            <span class="circle" style="border-color:#d73449;background-color:#d73449;"></span>
                            <h3>Today</h3>
                                                    <p class="text">In addition to digitizing Buddhist texts in Tibetan, Mongolian, Khmer, Burmese and other languages, BDRC works at the cutting edge of the digital humanities, including machine translation of Tibetan, handwritten text recognition, and advanced processing of etexts.</p>

</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid " style='background-color:#efefef; text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix" style='padding: 0% 10%'><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-1/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder one_column responsive_mode_from_1300' ><div class='q_elements_item ' data-768-1024='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-600-768='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480-600='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-480='40px 0px 0px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_869403' style='vertical-align:top;text-align:left;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_869403' style='padding:80px 0px 0px 0px'><h2 style="text-align: left" class="vc_custom_heading" >History</h2></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12 vc_col-lg-4/5"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div      class="vc_row wpb_row section vc_row-fluid vc_inner " style=' text-align:left;'><div class=" full_section_inner clearfix"><div class="wpb_column vc_column_container vc_col-sm-12"><div class="vc_column-inner"><div class="wpb_wrapper"><div class='q_elements_holder two_columns responsive_mode_from_1300' ><div class='q_elements_item ' data-1024-1280='90px 0px 0px 0px' data-768-1024='25px 0px 0px 0px' data-600-768='25px 0px 0px 0px' data-480-600='25px 0px 0px 0px' data-480='25px 0px 0px 0px' data-animation='no' data-item-class='q_elements_holder_custom_749140' style='vertical-align:top;'><div class='q_elements_item_inner'><div class='q_elements_item_content q_elements_holder_custom_749140' style='padding:90px 20px 80px 0px'>
	<div class="wpb_text_column wpb_content_element ">
		<div class="wpb_wrapper">
			<p>BDRC was founded as the Tibetan Buddhist Resource Center in 1999 by E. Gene Smith (1936-2010). Since its founding, BDRC has located, digitized, cataloged, and archived over 27 million pages of culturally significant works of Buddhist literature. BDRC stores the works in its secure archive, built to ensure that the texts remain free and accessible to the public into the indefinite future.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>Guided by Gene Smith's unique vision, BDRC has raised the once critically endangered Tibetan literary corpus to a high level of preservation. Today, BDRC's library is a crucial resource for Tibetan studies, relied on by an international community of academic scholars, religious leaders, translators, Tibetan scholars, publishers, and the interested public.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>In 2015, at the request of its Board of Directors, partners, and collaborators in the field of Buddhist studies, the Tibetan Buddhist Resource Center expanded its mission and, in 2016, officially became the Buddhist Digital Resource Center. In addition to continuing the work started by E. Gene Smith, BDRC is now tasked with preserving and disseminating all Buddhist literary traditions that remain endangered due to precarious social, political, and environmental factors.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>BDRC applies the technological expertise and scholarly resources it has cultivated for nearly two decades to this most pressing task.</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>
<p>&nbsp;</p>

<script id="bdrc-living-topbar-js">
(function (data) {
	if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains(data.cssClass)) {
		return;
	}

	function syncOffset() {
		var content = document.querySelector('.content.content_top_margin');
		var bar = document.querySelector('.bdrc-living-topbar');
		var header = document.querySelector('header.page_header') || document.querySelector('header');
		if (!content || !bar || !header) {
			return;
		}
		content.style.removeProperty('margin-top');
		// Below the theme's mobile breakpoint the header sits in normal flow and
		// pushes the content down on its own — a margin would just open a gap.
		if (getComputedStyle(header).position !== 'fixed') {
			return;
		}
		// Measured rather than derived: the theme's own margin is a constant tied
		// to its 105px bar, and the skin changes that height.
		// The theme declares this margin with !important, so ours has to match.
		content.style.setProperty('margin-top', header.offsetHeight + 'px', 'important');
	}

	function mount() {
		var holder = document.querySelector('.header_top_bottom_holder');
		var bottom = document.querySelector('.header_bottom');
		if (!holder || !bottom || document.querySelector('.bdrc-living-topbar')) {
			return;
		}

		var bar = document.createElement('div');
		bar.className = 'bdrc-living-topbar';

		var inner = document.createElement('div');
		inner.className = 'bdrc-living-topbar__inner';

		var tagline = document.createElement('span');
		tagline.className = 'bdrc-living-topbar__tagline';
		tagline.textContent = data.tagline;

		var nav = document.createElement('nav');
		nav.className = 'bdrc-living-topbar__links';
		data.links.forEach(function (link) {
			var a = document.createElement('a');
			a.href = link.url;
			a.textContent = link.label;
			if (link.class) {
				a.className = link.class;
			}
			nav.appendChild(a);
		});

		inner.appendChild(tagline);
		inner.appendChild(nav);
		bar.appendChild(inner);
		holder.insertBefore(bar, bottom);
		syncOffset();
	}

	/**
	 * Tags section rows on the pages the design does not cover, so the palette
	 * remap in generic_sections_css can hook onto them.
	 *
	 * Read from `element.style.backgroundColor` rather than matched with a CSS
	 * attribute selector because the inline notation is not stable — the same
	 * colour appears as `#d73449` on one row and `rgb(215, 52, 73)` on another.
	 * The CSSOM always normalises to rgb().
	 */
	var BANDS = {
		'rgb(242, 242, 242)': 'paper',
		'rgb(239, 239, 239)': 'paper',
		'rgb(230, 230, 230)': 'paper',
		'rgb(255, 255, 255)': 'paper',
		'rgb(252, 224, 142)': 'sand',
		'rgb(215, 52, 73)': 'dark'
	};

	function classifyBands() {
		var body = document.body;
		if (!body.classList.contains('bdrc-skin')) {
			return;
		}
		// The designed pages carry their own rules; WPML keeps the original
		// page-id class on translations, so this holds in every language.
		if (body.classList.contains('home')) {
			return;
		}
		var designed = ['page-id-24', 'page-id-26', 'page-id-28', 'page-id-30', 'page-id-33', 'page-id-35', 'page-id-37'];
		for (var i = 0; i < designed.length; i++) {
			if (body.classList.contains(designed[i])) {
				return;
			}
		}
		var rows = document.querySelectorAll('.full_width_inner > .vc_row');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(rows, function (row) {
			var band = BANDS[row.style.backgroundColor];
			if (band) {
				row.classList.add('bdrc-band-' + band);
			}
		});
	}

	var resizeTimer;
	window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
		clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
		resizeTimer = setTimeout(syncOffset, 150);
	});

	/**
	 * Repaints the theme's pictograms when they land on a light band.
	 *
	 * They are SVGs drawn in white (`#FFFFFF`, with a `#EFEFEF` ring) for the red
	 * and grey bands the theme used to have. Wherever the skin repaints a band
	 * light — the "support / library" cards on About and Archive, the remapped
	 * bands on the pages the design does not cover — a white pictogram leaves just
	 * its pale ring: the "empty circle" the page seems to be missing an icon for.
	 *
	 * A CSS filter cannot recolour to an exact palette value, so the shape is used
	 * as a mask on a span painted with the palette colour, and the original image
	 * is hidden behind it. Done in JS because the mask needs each image's own URL,
	 * which CSS has no way to read.
	 *
	 * Dark bands keep the CSS knockout to cream they already had.
	 */
	function repaintPictos() {
		if (!document.body.classList.contains('bdrc-skin')) {
			return;
		}
		if (!CSS.supports('mask-image', 'url(a.svg)') && !CSS.supports('-webkit-mask-image', 'url(a.svg)')) {
			return;
		}

		function luminance(color) {
			var m = color.match(/[\d.]+/g);
			if (!m) {
				return null;
			}
			if (m[3] !== undefined && Number(m[3]) < 0.1) {
				return null;
			}
			return (0.2126 * m[0] + 0.7152 * m[1] + 0.0722 * m[2]) / 255;
		}

		// The same bands leave the theme's inline `color:#ffffff` on the copy of
		// these blocks — white on a white card, so the second half of each title
		// ("support BDRC", "library, BUDA") simply vanished. Marked here and
		// recoloured in CSS, by the same light-or-dark test.
		var blocks = document.querySelectorAll('.full_width_inner .q_icon_with_title');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(blocks, function (block) {
			var node = block.parentElement;
			var lum = null;
			while (node && lum === null) {
				lum = luminance(getComputedStyle(node).backgroundColor);
				node = node.parentElement;
			}
			if (lum !== null && lum >= 0.5) {
				block.classList.add('bdrc-iwt-light');
			}
		});

		var imgs = document.querySelectorAll('.full_width_inner .icon_holder img');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(imgs, function (img) {
			if (!/\.svg(\?|$)/i.test(img.getAttribute('src') || '') || img.dataset.bdrcPicto) {
				return;
			}
			// nearest painted ancestor decides light or dark
			var node = img.parentElement;
			var lum = null;
			while (node && lum === null) {
				lum = luminance(getComputedStyle(node).backgroundColor);
				node = node.parentElement;
			}
			if (lum === null || lum < 0.5) {
				return;
			}

			var shape = document.createElement('span');
			shape.className = 'bdrc-picto';
			var url = 'url("' + img.src + '")';
			shape.style.webkitMaskImage = url;
			shape.style.maskImage = url;
			img.dataset.bdrcPicto = 'repainted';
			img.style.display = 'none';
			img.parentElement.insertBefore(shape, img);
		});
	}

	/**
	 * Points the library links at the development host while the skin is on.
	 *
	 * The theme renders these links in its menus and page content, so they cannot
	 * be set in PHP without freezing a staging URL into pages that full-page
	 * caching then serves to everyone. Done here, nothing is rewritten unless the
	 * skin is actually on. Only the exact host is swapped, so the pass is
	 * idempotent and leaves every other bdrc.io link alone.
	 */
	function retargetLibraryLinks() {
		if (!data.libraryFrom || !data.libraryTo || data.libraryFrom === data.libraryTo) {
			return;
		}
		var links = document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="' + data.libraryFrom + '"]');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(links, function (a) {
			var url;
			try {
				url = new URL(a.href, location.href);
			} catch (e) {
				return;
			}
			if (url.hostname !== data.libraryFrom) {
				return;
			}
			url.hostname = data.libraryTo;
			a.href = url.toString();
		});
	}

	function boot() {
		mount();
		classifyBands();
		repaintPictos();
		retargetLibraryLinks();
	}

	// This script is printed at `wp_footer`, so the header markup is already in
	// the document — waiting for DOMContentLoaded only delayed the strip past the
	// first paint. Mount straight away when the holder is there, and keep the
	// listener as the fallback for anything that moves it later.
	if (document.querySelector('.header_top_bottom_holder')) {
		boot();
	} else if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
	} else {
		boot();
	}

(function (data) {
	if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains(data.cssClass)) {
		return;
	}

	var host = document.querySelector('footer .footer_inner') || document.querySelector('footer');
	if (!host || host.querySelector('.bdrc-living-footer')) {
		return;

	var f = data.footer;

	function el(tag, cls, html) {
		var node = document.createElement(tag);
		if (cls) { node.className = cls; }
		if (html != null) { node.innerHTML = html; }
		return node;
	}

	function link(item, cls) {
		var a = el('a', cls || '', item.label);
		a.href = item.url;
		return a;
	}

	var root = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer');
	var grid = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-grid');

	var brand = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-brand');

	brand.appendChild(el('p', 'bdrc-living-footer-blurb', f.blurb));
	grid.appendChild(brand);

	f.columns.forEach(function (col) {
		var wrap = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-col');
		wrap.appendChild(el('h4', '', col.heading));
		if (col.html) {
			wrap.appendChild(el('p', '', col.html));
		}
		if (col.button) {
			wrap.appendChild(link(col.button, 'bdrc-living-footer-button'));
		}
		if (col.links) {
			var list = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-links');
			col.links.forEach(function (item) { list.appendChild(link(item)); });
			wrap.appendChild(list);
		}
		grid.appendChild(wrap);
	});

	// links. Rather than drop them, the theme's own icon markup is *moved* into
	// the brand column — moved, not copied, so hiding the old widgets below does
	// not take them with it, and no label is invented.
	var social = host.querySelectorAll('.q_social_icon_holder');
	if (social.length) {
		var row = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-social');
		Array.prototype.forEach.call(social, function (node) { row.appendChild(node); });
		brand.appendChild(row);
	}

	root.appendChild(grid);

	// The rule above the bottom bar runs edge to edge in the design while its
	// content stays on the 1440 measure — so it needs its own inner box.
	var bar = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-bar');
	var barInner = el('div', 'bdrc-living-footer-bar-inner');
	barInner.appendChild(el('span', '', f.legal));
	barInner.appendChild(link(f.privacy));
	bar.appendChild(barInner);
	root.appendChild(bar);

	host.insertBefore(root, host.firstChild);

	// Only now that the replacement is in place.
	['.footer_top_holder', '.footer_bottom_holder'].forEach(function (sel) {
		var node = host.querySelector(sel);
		if (node) { node.style.display = 'none'; }
	});
